SHRD (M16, R16, CL) - Latency

Operands

  • Operand 1 (r/w): Memory
  • Operand 2 (r): Register (AX, CX, DX, BX, SP, BP, SI, DI, R8W, R9W, R10W, R11W, R12W, R13W, R14W, R15W)
  • Operand 3 (r): Register (CL)
  • Operand 4 (w, suppressed): Flags (AF: undef, CF: cw, OF: undef, PF: cw, SF: cw, ZF: cw)

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤12

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, index register): ≤12

Latency operand 1 → 1 (memory): 14

Latency operand 1 → 4 (address, base register): 12

Latency operand 1 → 4 (address, index register): 12

Latency operand 1 → 4 (memory): ≤10

Latency operand 2 → 1: ≤10

Latency operand 2 → 4: 4

Latency operand 3 → 1: ≤11

Latency operand 3 → 4: 6

Latency operand 4 → 1: ≤13

Latency operand 4 → 4: 4

Latency operand 1 → 1 (address, base register): ≤12

  • Chain instructions: MOV R12W, [R14];MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;MOVSX R12, R12W;XOR R14, R12; XOR R14, R12

  • Instruction: SHRD word ptr [R14], R8W, CL
  • Chain instruction: CMOVP R14, R14
  • Chain latency: 2
  • Code: 
       0:	66 45 0f ad 06       	shrd   WORD PTR [r14],r8w,cl
   5:	4d 0f 4a f6          	cmovp  r14,r14
  • Init: 
    MOV R8W, 0;
MOV CL, 0
  • Show nanoBench command
  • Results:
    • Instructions retired: 2.0
    • Core cycles: 14.72
    • Reference cycles: 15.04
    • UOPS_RETIRED.ALL: 15.0

Experiment 2 (with R8W=0, with CL=0, with dependency-breaking instructions)

  • Instruction: SHRD word ptr [R14], R8W, CL
  • Chain instructions: CMOVP R14, R14;TEST R15, R15
  • Chain latency: 2
  • Code: 
       0:	66 45 0f ad 06       	shrd   WORD PTR [r14],r8w,cl
   5:	4d 0f 4a f6          	cmovp  r14,r14
   9:	4d 85 ff             	test   r15,r15
  • Init: 
    MOV R8W, 0;
MOV CL, 0
  • Show nanoBench command
  • Results:
    • Instructions retired: 3.0
    • Core cycles: 14.86
    • Reference cycles: 14.88
    • UOPS_RETIRED.ALL: 16.0
